| B-set-nominal | USBL-like + depth | +1.00 | +0.99 | +0.99 | +0.99 | +0.99 | +1.00 | **+0.993** | 0.001 | 0 | ✅ |
| B-set-2d | horizontal fixes + depth | +1.00 | +0.97 | +1.00 | +0.99 | +1.00 | +1.00 | **+0.991** | 0.001 | 0 | ✅ |
